class="gc-gap-label">What\'s Missing</div>\n          <div class="gc-gap-text">Accessible authentication that works on older devices or offers fallback methods (SMS, hardware tokens, web-based verification) without forcing hardware upgrades. Disproportionately affects low-income, elderly, and rural users.</div>\n        </div>\n        <div class="gc-opportunity">\n          <div class="gc-opp-label">The Opportunity</div>\n          <div class="gc-opp-text">A 2FA platform that supports multiple fallback methods alongside app-based auth, with automatic degradation to accessible alternatives for devices that can\'t run the latest OS — no user left behind by a software update cycle they can\'t control.</div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <!-- Gap 2 -->\n      <div class="gap-card" data-sector="events">\n        <div class="gc-header">\n          <div class="gc-title-group">\n            <div class="gc-num">Gap #02</div>\n            <div class="gc-title">Wedding Tech — Subscription Model Mismatch</div>\n          </div>\n          <div class="gc-badges">\n            <span class="badge badge-sector">Events</span>\n            <span class="badge badge-score">500 signal</span>\n            <span class="badge badge-opp">Validated Gap</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div class="gc-complaint">"Nobody wants to pay monthly for something they need for 6 months. We almost launched with a SaaS model. Switched to one-time event passes ($69–$149) and conversions jumped immediately."</div>\n        <div class="gc-gap">\n          <div class="gc-gap-label">What\'s Missing</div>\n          <div class="gc-gap-text">Pricing models aligned with short-term, event-based needs. Existing platforms treat weddings like ongoing subscriptions when they\'re single-event purchases. The market has validated the fix — founders just need to adopt it.</div>\n        </div>\n        <div class="gc-opportunity">\n          <div class="gc-opp-label">The Opportunity</div>\n          <div class="gc-opp-text">Wedding-focused tools offered as one-time event passes or tiered packages tied to the event date. The conversion improvement is already documented — this is an execution gap, not a discovery gap.</div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <!-- Gap 3 -->\n      <div class="gap-card" data-sector="consumer">\n        <div class="gc-header">\n          <div class="gc-title-group">\n            <div class="gc-num">Gap #03</div>\n            <div class="gc-title">Subscription Creep — No Perpetual License Option</div>\n          </div>\n          <div class="gc-badges">\n            <span class="badge badge-sector">Consumer</span>\n            <span class="badge badge-score">110 signal</span>\n            <span class="badge badge-opp">Emerging Gap</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div class="gc-complaint">"Entire things you used to own are now leased to you for more money and your private data — software, music, video games, features in your new car, etc. It\'s actually shameful, but difficult to fight."</div>\n        <div class="gc-gap">\n          <div class="gc-gap-label">What\'s Missing</div>\n          <div class="gc-gap-text">Transparent ownership options or fair-value lease-to-own alternatives that respect user control and data privacy. An "anti-subscription" brand positioning could command significant premium.</div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <!-- Gap 4 -->\n      <div class="gap-card" data-sector="b2b">\n        <div class="gc-header">\n          <div class="gc-title-group">\n            <div class="gc-num">Gap #04</div>\n            <div class="gc-title">Customer Service Training — Panic-Prone Instructors</div>\n          </div>\n          <div class="gc-badges">\n            <span class="badge badge-sector">B2B Tools</span>\n            <span class="badge badge-score">20 signal</span>\n            <span class="badge badge-opp">Niche Gap</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div class="gc-complaint">"From day one of training, there were constant technical issues. Every single day someone had system problems. Instead of handling the situation calmly and professionally, the trainer would panic and get frustrated."</div>\n        <div class="gc-gap">\n          <div class="gc-gap-label">What\'s Missing</div>\n          <div class="gc-gap-text">Reliable, low-tech training environments and instructors trained in de-escalation. Current platforms simulate perfect conditions but not real-world failure scenarios. Instructors aren\'t coached on composure under technical failure.</div>\n        </div>\n        <div class="gc-opportunity">\n          <div class="gc-opp-label">The Opportunity</div>\n          <div class="gc-opp-text">A customer service training platform that deliberately simulates technical failures, coaches instructors on de-escalation protocols, and scores trainers on composure — not just content delivery. The training for the trainers is the gap.</div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <!-- Gap 5 -->\n      <div class="gap-card" data-sector="b2b">\n        <div class="gc-header">\n          <div class="gc-title-group">\n            <div class="gc-num">Gap #05</div>\n            <div class="gc-title">Manufacturer Communication — Unilateral Design Changes</div>\n          </div>\n          <div class="gc-badges">\n            <span class="badge badge-sector">B2B Tools</span>\n            <span class="badge badge-score">9 signal</span>\n            <span class="badge badge-opp">Niche Gap</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div class="gc-complaint">"When I asked my supplier, they said it\'s because these details are difficult to produce. Well I understand there\'s a gap between design and production, while the point is, they didn\'t ask me anything before they made this decision!"</div>\n        <div class="gc-gap">\n          <div class="gc-gap-label">What\'s Missing</div>\n          <div class="gc-gap-text">Proactive collaboration loops where manufacturers seek client approval before altering prototypes. No standard change-request workflow exists between small clients and overseas manufacturers — the power imbalance is built into the process.</div>\n        </div>\n        <div class="gc-opportunity">\n          <div class="gc-opp-label">The Opportunity</div>\n          <div class="gc-opp-text">A prototype-management tool that enforces change-request workflows, version control, and automated client notifications for any deviation from spec — essentially GitHub for physical product development between founders and manufacturers.</div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <!-- Gap 6 -->\n      <div class="gap-card" data-sector="proptech">\n        <div class="gc-header">\n          <div class="gc-title-group">\n            <div class="gc-num">Gap #06</div>\n            <div class="gc-title">Smart Locks — No Tenant-Only Privacy Mode</div>\n          </div>\n          <div class="gc-badges">\n            <span class="badge badge-sector">PropTech</span>\n            <span class="badge badge-score">900 signal</span>\n            <span class="badge badge-opp">High Opportunity</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div class="gc-complaint">"If they have access to the app, they can remotely lock/unlock the door. As well as see when the door is locked/unlocked. Essentially trolling to see when you come and go. I\'d be pissed."</div>\n        <div class="gc-gap">\n          <div class="gc-gap-label">What\'s Missing</div>\n          <div class="gc-gap-text">Tenant-controlled access logs and the ability to opt out of landlord remote management while maintaining security. Current smart lock systems are designed for property owner control, not tenant privacy rights.</div>\n        </div>\n        <div class="gc-opportunity">\n          <div class="gc-opp-label">The Opportunity</div>\n          <div class="gc-opp-text">Smart locks with a verified tenant-only mode that disables remote landlord access and provides transparent, real-time access logs visible only to the tenant. A tenant-rights positioning in the smart home market that no current player occupies.</div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <!-- Gap 7 -->\n      <div class="gap-card" data-sector="hr">\n        <div class="gc-header">\n          <div class="gc-title-group">\n            <div class="gc-num">Gap #07</div>\n            <div class="gc-title">Remote Job Boards — Scam Signal vs. Legitimate Work</div>\n          </div>\n          <div class="gc-badges">\n            <span class="badge badge-sector">HR & Hiring</span>\n            <span class="badge badge-score">250 signal</span>\n            <span class="badge badge-opp">High Opportunity</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div class="gc-complaint">"I see a lot of advice about remote work and online skills, but it\'s hard to know what actually helps you get real work."</div>\n        <div class="gc-gap">\n          <div class="gc-gap-label">What\'s Missing</div>\n          <div class="gc-gap-text">Curated, vetted job listings that signal legitimacy, with clear skill-to-job matching for entry-level candidates. Current boards are flooded with scams, and legitimate listings are indistinguishable from fraudulent ones to new market entrants.</div>\n        </div>\n        <div class="gc-opportunity">\n          <div class="gc-opp-label">The Opportunity</div>\n          <div class="gc-opp-text">A job board that verifies employers, highlights entry-level-friendly roles, and includes skill-assessment pathways so beginners can prove competence before applying — removing the chicken-and-egg problem of needing experience to get experience.</div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <!-- Gap 8 -->\n      <div class="gap-card" data-sector="consumer">\n        <div class="gc-header">\n          <div class="gc-title-group">\n            <div class="gc-num">Gap #08</div>\n            <div class="gc-title">Kitchen Station Hygiene — No Shift Handover Accountability</div>\n          </div>\n          <div class="gc-badges">\n            <span class="badge badge-sector">Consumer</span>\n            <span class="badge badge-score">250 signal</span>\n            <span class="badge badge-opp">Quick Win</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div class="gc-complaint">"That\'s the current problem I have with my expo station. I go in 30 mins early every day just to clean up after lunch shift."</div>\n        <div class="gc-gap">\n          <div class="gc-gap-label">What\'s Missing</div>\n          <div class="gc-gap-text">Enforced cleanup protocols or automated reminders that ensure stations are left ready for the next user.
